# Path map

Directories were renamed twice: on 2026-09-28 to match the volume numbers, and on 2026-09-29, when Vol.2 and Vol.3 swapped places so that Nemotron on NIM (Vol.2) comes before the judges (Vol.3). Files written before a rename, including frozen pre-registrations, refer to the paths of their day; this file maps every one through both renames. To reproduce a run byte for byte, check out the tag listed for its volume. Check out a tag or the tip of the branch; a pure-rename commit alone is not a supported checkout (its .gitattributes still names the paths before it).

| Volume | Directory | Reproduce with |
|---|---|---|
| Vol.1 | `vol1-nim/` | `git checkout vol1-nim-published` |
| Vol.2 | `vol2-nemotron/` | `git checkout vol2-nemotron-published` |
| Vol.3 | `vol3-judges/` | `git checkout vol3-judges-baseline` |

Generated by `tools/renumber_dirs.py` from `git diff -M100% --name-status` of each rename commit (2026-09-28: subject "Rename the volume directories to match the volume numbers (moves only)", tree `cac49b8e310f`, 1832 rows; 2026-09-29: subject "Swap the Vol.2 and Vol.3 directories to follow the new volume order (moves only)", tree `1ff1b20542ea`, 1228 rows). Every row of every rename is a pure move (`R100`). 1832 paths; `PATH_MAP.json` holds the same rows.
